Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on enzymes structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 436. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 395 with relations in degree 2.

For the inductive step, we decompose the enzymes structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 395.
